import torch
import torch.nn as nn

NUM_TOKENS = 512
NUM_HEADS = 32
NUM_KV_HEADS = 8
HEAD_SIZE = 128
ROT_DIM = 64

def get_inputs():
    q = torch.randn(NUM_TOKENS, NUM_HEADS, HEAD_SIZE, dtype=torch.float16).cuda()
    k = torch.randn(NUM_TOKENS, NUM_KV_HEADS, HEAD_SIZE, dtype=torch.float16).cuda()
    cos = torch.randn(NUM_TOKENS, ROT_DIM, dtype=torch.float16).cuda()
    sin = torch.randn(NUM_TOKENS, ROT_DIM, dtype=torch.float16).cuda()
    return [q, k, cos, sin]

def get_init_inputs():
    return [ROT_DIM]

class ModelNew(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, rot_dim):
        super().__init__()
        self.rot_dim = rot_dim
        import torch.utils.cpp_extension as ext

        cpp_source = """
#include <torch/extension.h>
void rotary_embedding_forward(
    torch::Tensor q,
    torch::Tensor k,
    torch::Tensor cos,
    torch::Tensor sin,
    int rot_dim);
"""

        cuda_source = r"""
#include <torch/extension.h>
#include <ATen/cuda/CUDAContext.h>
#include <cuda_runtime.h>

using scalar_t = at::Half;

__global__ void rotary_embedding_kernel(
    scalar_t* __restrict__ q,
    scalar_t* __restrict__ k,
    const scalar_t* __restrict__ cos,
    const scalar_t* __restrict__ sin,
    int num_tokens,
    int num_heads,
    int num_kv_heads,
    int head_size,
    int rot_dim,
    int64_t q_stride_t,
    int64_t q_stride_h,
    int64_t k_stride_t,
    int64_t k_stride_h,
    int64_t cs_stride_t) {

    const int token = blockIdx.x;
    if (token >= num_tokens) return;

    const int embed_dim = rot_dim >> 1;

    extern __shared__ char smem[];
    scalar_t* cos_smem = reinterpret_cast<scalar_t*>(smem);
    scalar_t* sin_smem = cos_smem + ((embed_dim + 7) & ~7);

    const scalar_t* cos_g = cos + token * cs_stride_t;
    const scalar_t* sin_g = sin + token * cs_stride_t;

    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < embed_dim; i += blockDim.x) {
        cos_smem[i] = cos_g[i];
        sin_smem[i] = sin_g[i];
    }
    __syncthreads();

    const int total_q = num_heads * embed_dim;
    for (int idx = threadIdx.x; idx < total_q; idx += blockDim.x) {
        const int head = idx / embed_dim;
        const int pair = idx % embed_dim;

        scalar_t* base = q + token * q_stride_t + head * q_stride_h;
        const float x = static_cast<float>(base[pair]);
        const float y = static_cast<float>(base[pair + embed_dim]);
        const float c = static_cast<float>(cos_smem[pair]);
        const float s = static_cast<float>(sin_smem[pair]);

        base[pair] = static_cast<scalar_t>(x * c - y * s);
        base[pair + embed_dim] = static_cast<scalar_t>(y * c + x * s);
    }

    const int total_k = num_kv_heads * embed_dim;
    for (int idx = threadIdx.x; idx < total_k; idx += blockDim.x) {
        const int head = idx / embed_dim;
        const int pair = idx % embed_dim;

        scalar_t* base = k + token * k_stride_t + head * k_stride_h;
        const float x = static_cast<float>(base[pair]);
        const float y = static_cast<float>(base[pair + embed_dim]);
        const float c = static_cast<float>(cos_smem[pair]);
        const float s = static_cast<float>(sin_smem[pair]);

        base[pair] = static_cast<scalar_t>(x * c - y * s);
        base[pair + embed_dim] = static_cast<scalar_t>(y * c + x * s);
    }
}

void rotary_embedding_forward(
    torch::Tensor q,
    torch::Tensor k,
    torch::Tensor cos,
    torch::Tensor sin,
    int rot_dim) {

    const int num_tokens = q.size(0);
    const int num_heads = q.size(1);
    const int head_size = q.size(2);
    const int num_kv_heads = k.size(1);

    const int64_t q_stride_t = q.stride(0);
    const int64_t q_stride_h = q.stride(1);
    const int64_t k_stride_t = k.stride(0);
    const int64_t k_stride_h = k.stride(1);
    const int64_t cs_stride_t = cos.stride(0);

    dim3 grid(num_tokens);
    dim3 block(256);
    const size_t smem_size = 2 * ((rot_dim / 2 + 7) & ~7) * sizeof(scalar_t);

    cudaStream_t stream = at::cuda::getCurrentCUDAStream();

    rotary_embedding_kernel<<<grid, block, smem_size, stream>>>(
        q.data_ptr<scalar_t>(),
        k.data_ptr<scalar_t>(),
        cos.data_ptr<scalar_t>(),
        sin.data_ptr<scalar_t>(),
        num_tokens,
        num_heads,
        num_kv_heads,
        head_size,
        rot_dim,
        q_stride_t,
        q_stride_h,
        k_stride_t,
        k_stride_h,
        cs_stride_t);
}
"""

        self._op = ext.load_inline(
            name="rotary_embedding_hopper_v2",
            cpp_sources=cpp_source,
            cuda_sources=cuda_source,
            functions=["rotary_embedding_forward"],
            extra_cuda_cflags=[
                "-O3",
                "--use_fast_math",
                "-gencode=arch=compute_90,code=sm_90",
            ],
            verbose=False,
        )

    def forward(self, q, k, cos, sin):
        q = q.contiguous()
        k = k.contiguous()
        cos = cos.contiguous()
        sin = sin.contiguous()
        self._op.rotary_embedding_forward(q, k, cos, sin, self.rot_dim)
        return q, k
